    // prev system: '0.60.143',   // [0.60.143: James: "We need persistent data look at the last commit." TL1b (docs/2026-10-16-durable-work-timeline-phasemap.spec addendum d). Found: 0.60.142 streamed the agent's thinking and reply into the Code tab, but every frame lived only in the page (app.js AGENT_FEED) and in lib/repo-activity.js's memory; guardian's response-sink writes the .response only when the reply ends. A reload, an Idearium restart or a Clear Glass close mid-reply lost the reply and the thinking so far — the .response half of the 17-chunk loss. Built: lib/work-journal.js keeps each repo agent job on disk as it streams, data/work/<repo>/<job>/reply.response (a .response node, rewritten atomically on every frame that changes it; a dom pulse is not a write) + timeline.jsonl (one line per event, no text). Both feed doors journal (guardian's onFeed, Ollama's POST agent/stream); GET /api/repos/:uuid/agent/feed[?job=] reads it back (an open job silent 15 min reads stale, said); `repo feed <repo> [job]`; the page restores the newest job when it opens ("restored from disk · <status>"), and a live frame wins. Proof: test-work-journal 3/3 (WJ-01 back byte-identical after a restart; WJ-03 in Clear Glass), test-agent-live 5/5, test-live-everywhere 3/3, test-plan-pieces 4/4, interaction-contract 39/39, test-build-surface 13/13. Also mapped (nothing built): the product for debuggin
    ```

    ```
